Purpose\n14\t\n15\tAuthor the **inputs + expects** body for eval E14 — the eleventh\n16\tpost-OQ-2 hook-surface coverage entry (R-086 … R-098), pinning the\n17\tharness's **SessionStart concurrency contract** (design-spec §11 /\n18\tOQ-2 D-0082 §4 row E14). E14 is the only entry in the v1 roster that\n19\texercises a **multi-session** surface: N=3 sessions spawned in rapid\n20\tsuccession (within ~200ms of each other) MUST each write their own\n21\tper-session sticky `state/auggie-first-pending/<sid>.txt` (no\n22\tcross-contamination) and each MUST emit exactly one `session_init`\n23\tevent in its own per-eval HOME's JSONL ledger.\n24\t\n25\tThe regression class motivating E14 is **shared-mutable-state at the\n26\tSessionStart boundary**: a hook script or harness path that writes\n27\tto a fixed location (no `<sid>` in the path) would have N=3 sessions\n28\trace on the same file, surfacing only the last writer's sticky and\n29\tcorrupting per-session telemetry. E14 pins the no-shared-state\n30\tinvariant.\n31\t\n32\tE14 is structurally distinct from E3 / E4 (which pin SINGLE-session\n33\tSessionStart hook fire) along one axis: **N=3 multi-session\n34\torchestration**. The OQ-2 contract (D-0082 §4 row E14 + D-4 decision\n35\tnotes) names the v1 implementation mechanism as the **YAML\n36\t`callback:` escape hatch** — a programmatic spawn ordering that\n37\tcannot be expressed in the declarative input/expects DSL. The\n38\tcallback (named `superclaude.cli.eval.suites.real_callbacks:\n39\tE14_concurrent_session_start` per D-0082 §4) is responsible for:\n40\t\n41\t  (a) spawning N=3 sessions in threads with ~200ms inter-arrival;\n42\t  (b) waiting for all 3 PTY sessions to reach prompt-ready;\n43\t  (c) collecting the 3 per-session HOMEs' sticky files + JSONL\n44\t      ledgers;\n45\t  (d) asserting no-shared-mutable-state — 3 distinct sticky files,\n46\t      3 distinct session_init JSONL rows, one per session.\n47\t\n48\tThe body must:\n49\t\n50\t- run on a fresh per-eval HOME isolated by FR-ISO2 (single-HOME\n51\t  proxy; the FULL N=3 multi-HOME variant lives behind the callback\n52\t  escape hatch);\n53\t- assert the SessionStart-boundary observable shape — sticky-pending\n54\t  directory created, session_init event row emitted, PTY teardown\n55\t  exit 0 — mapped to the available declarative DSL primitives\n56\t  (mapping documented in §3 and §8.1 below);\n57\t- exit cleanly so `exit_code.equals(0)` can pin the PTY teardown\n58\t  contract — sibling to E3-E13;\n59\t- carry no capability tag (`requires: []`) — pure PTY spawn; no\n60\t  MCP, no network. Matches OQ-2 D-0082 §6 row E14.\n61\t\n62\tThe \"YAML `callback:` field\" + the \"multi-session orchestration\"\n63\tcannot be expressed in the current declarative DSL **or** the\n64\tcurrent schema (`evalEntry.additionalProperties: false` rejects the\n65\t`callback:` key). The strict form is deferred to follow-up tasks\n66\tper the established T05.07..T05.19 precedent: land the OQ-2 body\n67\tverbatim with the best-effort declarative N=1 proxy, document the\n68\tgap in §8.1, and gate the strict form on the schema-bump + loader-\n69\textension + EvalRunner callback-invocation wiring described in\n70\tD-4 / D-0082 §4.\n71\t\n72\t## 2. Concurrency contract (from design-spec §11 + OQ-2 D-0082 §4)\n73\t\n74\tThe harness's SessionStart hook chain (`session-init.sh` +\n75\t`freshness-session-start.sh` per `src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json`)\n76\tmust produce per-session output keyed by `$SESSION_ID`:\n77\t\n78\t```\n79\tFor each spawned session i in {1..N=3}:\n80\t  1. PTY spawn fires SessionStart hooks before prompt-ready.\n81\t  2. session-init.sh writes state/session-init.log under <home_i>.\n82\t  3. freshness-session-start.sh writes\n83\t     state/auggie-first-pending/<sid_i>.txt under <home_i>.\n84\t  4. session-init.sh (per OQ-2 contract) appends\n85\t     `{\"type\":\"session_init\", \"sid\":<sid_i>, ...}` to\n86\t     <home_i>/.claude/logs/session-events.jsonl.\n87\t\n88\tMulti-session invariants:\n89\t  - Each home_i is a DISTINCT per-eval HOME (no cross-pollination).\n90\t  - The <sid_i> values are pairwise distinct.\n91\t  - Each logs/session-events.jsonl contains exactly ONE\n92\t    session_init row (event_count == 1 per session).\n93\t  - No state/auggie-first-pending/<sid_i>.txt appears under\n94\t    home_j for i != j.\n95\t```\n96\t\n97\tThe fixture spawn function (per OQ-2 D-0082 §4 row E14) is\n98\t`superclaude.cli.eval.suites.real_callbacks:E14_concurrent_session_start`\n99\t— a Python callable that uses threads + the PTY harness to drive\n100\tthe 3 spawns and collects per-session observables for assertion.\n101\t\n102\t| Observable | Purpose |\n103\t|---|---|\n104\t| `state/auggie-first-pending/<sid>.txt` (per session) | proves the sticky-pending mechanism is keyed by `<sid>` (no shared-mutable-state) |\n105\t| `logs/session-events.jsonl` (per session) contains `\"type\":\"session_init\"` | proves the session_init event row was emitted in EACH per-session ledger |\n106\t| `event_count == 1` per session ledger | proves no duplicate emission (the SessionStart hook chain fires exactly once per spawn) |\n107\t| `exit_code == 0` (per session) | proves clean PTY teardown for each spawn — the concurrent spawns don't corrupt each other's exit codepaths |\n108\t\n109\t## 3. Frozen body shape\n110\t\n111\tThe body lands in `suites/real.yaml` under the E14 entry that\n112\tpreviously carried only a stale placeholder (`title: \"doctor reports\n113\tMCP gateway reachability\"`, `category: doctor` — left over from the\n114\tpre-OQ-2 numbering when E14 was a doctor-surface eval). T05.20\n115\treplaces the scaffolding with the frozen body. Final shape:\n116\t\n117\t| Field | Value |\n118\t|---|---|\n119\t| **title** | `\"Concurrent SessionStart bursts\"` (matches D-0082 §4 OQ-2 row E14) |\n120\t| **category** | `hook-lifecycle` (sibling to E3-E13; was `doctor` in stale stub — the eval surface is the SessionStart hook chain at the multi-session boundary, which is part of the hook lifecycle, not the doctor diagnostics surface) |\n121\t| **requires** | `[]` — no capability tags; the callback (once landed) spawns local PTY sessions only |\n122\t| **timeout_sec** | `60` (matches E3-E13 sibling parity; in practice the degenerate N=1 proxy is bounded by 1× `/quit` through the PTY, typically <10s — 60s is generous headroom for the N=1 form. The full N=3 multi-session form would need a larger budget; that's a follow-up concern gated on the callback escape hatch landing.) |\n123\t| **inputs[0].prompt** | `\"/quit\"` — fresh PTY session that fires the SessionStart hook chain; immediate clean exit. The N=3 concurrent-spawn aspect is fully deferred to the callback escape hatch (per §3 schema-expressibility constraint below). |\n124\t| **expects[0]** | `file: { path: state/auggie-first-pending, exists: true }` — the sticky-pending directory created by `freshness-session-start.sh:91` on every SessionStart; necessary-but-not-sufficient proxy for \"each session writes its own `<sid>.txt`\" |\n125\t| **expects[1]** | `file: { path: logs/session-events.jsonl, exists: true, contains: '\"type\":\"session_init\"' }` — single-session degenerate of \"3 distinct session_init events\"; sibling pattern to E3 |\n126\t| **expects[2]** | `exit_code: { equals: 0 }` — clean PTY teardown |\n127\t\n128\tPTY-exclusion tag: `no_pty: skip` (carried forward from the\n129\tscaffolding entry — every eval in the `real` suite is PTY-driven\n130\tper DOC-OQ3 / R-077).\n131\t\n132\tNo additions to `optional_capabilities` — the callback (once\n133\tlanded) is a pure Python thread-fan-out + PTY spawn; no MCP server\n134\tis required.\n135\t\n136\t### Schema-expressibility constraint — the `callback:` field\n137\t\n138\tThe OQ-2 D-0082 §4 row E14 inputs shape names a **YAML `callback:`\n139\tfield** that invokes\n140\t`superclaude.cli.eval.suites.real_callbacks:E14_concurrent_session_start`\n141\tper the D-4 escape hatch. The current `suites/suite.schema.json`\n142\tshape for `evalEntry`:\n143\t\n144\t```json\n145\t\"evalEntry\": {\n146\t  \"required\": [\"id\", \"title\"],\n147\t  \"additionalProperties\": false,\n148\t  \"properties\": { \"id\": ..., \"title\": ..., \"category\": ...,\n149\t                  \"requires\": ..., \"timeout_sec\": ...,\n150\t                  \"isolation\": ..., \"inputs\": ..., \"expects\": ...,\n151\t                  \"parameterize\": ..., \"no_pty\": ... }\n152\t}\n153\t```\n154\t\n155\t`additionalProperties: false` rejects any unrecognised field; the\n156\tD-4 `callback:` escape hatch is documented in `decisions.md` (D-4)\n157\tbut **not yet wired** into the schema, the loader, OR the EvalRunner.\n158\tAdding the field end-to-end requires:\n159\t\n160\t  (a) `suite.schema.json` extension — add `callback: string` (or\n161\t      `callback: { module: string, function: string }`) to\n162\t      `evalEntry.properties`;\n163\t  (b) loader update — `SuiteLoader` resolves the callable from the\n164\t      `callback:` string by `importlib.import_module + getattr`;\n165\t  (c) EvalRunner update — when an EvalSpec carries a `callback`, the\n166\t      runner invokes it (instead of driving inputs through the PTY)\n167\t      and consumes the callback's returned outcome shape;\n168\t  (d) the `superclaude.cli.eval.suites.real_callbacks` module +\n169\t      `E14_concurrent_session_start` function — actual multi-session\n170\t      orchestration implementation.\n171\t\n172\tNone of (a)-(d) exist today. Per the established T05.07..T05.19\n173\tdeferral posture, T05.20 lands the OQ-2 body shape with the\n174\tbest-effort declarative proxy:\n175\t\n176\t- **N=3 → degenerate N=1.** A single PTY session (`inputs[0].prompt:\n177\t  \"/quit\"`) fires the SessionStart hook chain, creates the sticky-\n178\t  pending directory, and emits one session_init event — the same\n179\t  observable shape E3 / E4 pin for SINGLE-session SessionStart. The\n180\t  N=3 concurrency aspect (no-shared-mutable-state across parallel\n181\t  spawns) is fully deferred to the callback escape hatch.\n182\t- **per-`<sid>` sticky file → directory existence proxy.**\n183\t  `Expect.file` (expect.py:187-268) has no glob support and no\n184\t  `<sid>` template substitution — the per-session file\n185\t  `state/auggie-first-pending/<sid>.txt` cannot be asserted without\n186\t  knowing the session_id ahead of time. The directory\n187\t  `state/auggie-first-pending/` (created by\n188\t  `freshness-session-start.sh:91` on every SessionStart) is a\n189\t  necessary-but-not-sufficient proxy: directory presence proves the\n190\t  sticky-pending mechanism initialised; the actual per-session file\n191\t  presence (and the no-cross-contamination guarantee across N=3\n192\t  HOMEs) is deferred.\n193\t- **3 distinct session_init events → 1 substring assertion.**\n194\t  Inherits the same proxy posture as E3: `file(logs/session-events.jsonl,\n195\t  contains '\"type\":\"session_init\"')`. The 3-events-across-3-HOMEs\n196\t  aspect is deferred (multi-HOME assertion semantics not supported\n197\t  — Expect.file resolves against a single EvalContext.home_path per\n198\t  expect.py:187-268).\n199\t- **`jsonl.event_count == 1` per session → degenerate-N=1 implicit.**\n200\t  A single-session run produces exactly one session_init row by\n201\t  construction; the per-session count=1 invariant for the multi-\n202\t  session case is deferred to the callback escape hatch\n203\t  (declarative `jsonl.event_count` requires a Python callable per\n204\t  expect.py:269-369).\n205\t\n206\tThis proxy posture mirrors the deferral footprint established by\n207\tT05.07..T05.19: lands the OQ-2 named ledger paths verbatim with\n208\tsingle-session degenerate observables, defers the schema-bump-gated\n209\tstrict form to follow-up tasks. T05.20 differs from prior bodies in\n210\tONE structural respect: it depends on a schema EXTENSION (the\n211\t`callback:` field) rather than only on a hook-script wiring (E3-E5)\n212\tor a hooks.json variant + fixture (E13).\n213\t\n214\t### Scaffolding gap — five preconditions\n215\t\n216\tThe OQ-2 input shape requires:\n217\t\n218\t1. **The `callback:` field added to `suite.schema.json`** —\n219\t   today's schema (`evalEntry.additionalProperties: false`) rejects\n220\t   it.\n221\t2. **The loader resolving `callback:` strings to callables** — no\n222\t   import path resolution exists in `SuiteLoader`.\n223\t3. **The EvalRunner invoking the callback** — `runner.py` drives\n224\t   ONE PTY session per eval via `claude_process.py`; there is no\n225\t   alternative invocation path that hands control to a Python\n226\t   callable.\n227\t4. **The `superclaude.cli.eval.suites.real_callbacks` module\n228\t   existing** — the named callable `E14_concurrent_session_start`\n229\t   has no implementation; the module directory itself does not\n230\t   exist.\n231\t5. **Multi-HOME isolation orchestration** — the per-eval setup\n232\t   wrapper (NFR-ISO2 / T02.13) creates ONE HOME per eval; the\n233\t   callback needs to create N=3 distinct HOMEs (one per concurrent\n234\t   session) AND have Expect.file resolve assertions against ALL of\n235\t   them (or have the callback collect per-session observables and\n236\t   return them in a shape the reporter consumes).\n237\t\n238\tIn addition, T05.20 inherits two telemetry gaps from E3 / T05.07:\n239\t\n240\t6. **`session-init.sh` doesn't append to `logs/session-events.jsonl`\n241\t   today** — the OQ-2 body names this ledger path; the current\n242\t   `src/superclaude/hooks/scripts/session-init.sh` writes its own\n243\t   `state/session-init.log` but does not emit the\n244\t   `{\"type\":\"session_init\"}` JSONL row. Follow-up hook-script task\n245\t   (shared with E3) is responsible for the emission.\n246\t7. **`<sid>` keying on the sticky file is in\n247\t   `freshness-session-start.sh:108`** — the script DOES write\n248\t   `state/auggie-first-pending/$SESSION_ID.txt`, so the per-`<sid>`\n249\t   keying is correct. But the proxy at expects[0] checks DIRECTORY\n250\t   existence, not per-session file existence. The strict per-`<sid>`\n251\t   assertion form is the deferral (gap #5 above).\n252\t\n253\tUntil gaps #1-#5 land, E14's full multi-session execution path is\n254\tnot invocable. The degenerate N=1 proxy lands the OQ-2-named\n255\tobservables (sticky-pending directory + session_init event +\n256\tclean exit) so the regression-detection envelope is partially\n257\tcovered TODAY: a regression that breaks the SessionStart hook\n258\tchain entirely (no sticky directory, no JSONL ledger, non-zero\n259\tPTY exit) would surface via the proxy. The strict no-shared-\n260\tmutable-state assertion lives behind the callback escape hatch.\n261\t\n262\t§8.1 documents the deferral with the same \"schema-gap +\n263\timplementation-gap\" framing.\n264\t\n265\t### Why `inputs[0].prompt: \"/quit\"` (no `expect_tool_call`)\n266\t\n267\tThe N=1 proxy doesn't need to PIN a specific tool call (unlike E13\n268\twhich pinned `expect_tool_call: Read`). The SessionStart hooks fire\n269\tBEFORE any tool call is issued — they fire as part of the PTY\n270\tspawn handshake, not in response to any specific tool. The `/quit`\n271\tinput is sufficient to trigger the full SessionStart chain and\n272\texit cleanly.\n273\t\n274\t## 4. Eval id passes FR-SCH2\n275\t\n276\t`validate_eval_id` (FR-SCH2 / T01.05) requires the eval id match\n277\t`^[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9]*([0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?)?$`. The literal id `E14` is\n278\ttrivially accepted — `eval describe --suite real --eval E14` returns\n279\tthe full body and `eval list --json` continues to enumerate 17\n280\tevals under suite `real`.\n281\t\n282\t## 5. Determinism (3-run AC)\n283\t\n284\tThe body is deterministic on a clean per-eval HOME (D-0082 §2\n285\tconstraint 2 / per-task AC):\n286\t\n287\t- The SessionStart hook chain (session-init.sh +\n288\t  freshness-session-start.sh) fires the same way on every PTY\n289\t  spawn — same scripts, same matchers, same side-effects.\n290\t- The `state/auggie-first-pending/` directory is created\n291\t  unconditionally on every SessionStart (per\n292\t  freshness-session-start.sh:91) — no time-of-day or network\n293\t  dependencies.\n294\t- The asserted substring `\"type\":\"session_init\"` (once the gap-#6\n295\t  hook-script emission lands) is invariant across runs; the\n296\t  `ts` / `session_id` fields on the JSONL row, once emitted, are\n297\t  not asserted against.\n298\t- The `/quit` input causes an immediate clean exit (exit code 0)\n299\t  after PTY harness EOF.\n300\t- No shared-state dependencies — D-0082 §2 constraint 3 (no\n301\t  `CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`) is honored.\n302\t\n303\tThree consecutive `eval run --suite real --eval E14` invocations\n304\ton a clean HOME must therefore yield identical EvalOutcome\n305\tstatuses, which is the per-task acceptance criterion. (The\n306\tdeterminism contract for the full N=3 multi-session form, once\n307\tthe callback escape hatch lands, depends on the callback honoring\n308\tthe same no-time-of-day, no-network, no-shared-state discipline —\n309\tthe OQ-2 contract says \"spawned in rapid succession (within\n310\t~200ms of each other) via threads\" which is deterministic modulo\n311\twall-clock skew. Same posture as standard thread-fan-out tests in\n312\tpytest.)\n313\t\n314\t## 6. Schema validation\n315\t\n316\tThe body uses only manifest-supported constructs:\n317\t\n318\t- `inputs[].prompt: string` (additionalProperties: true under\n319\t  `evalEntry.inputs.items` per `suite.schema.json`).\n320\t- `expects[]` rows matching `{primitive: kwargs}` shape — resolved\n321\t  at load-time by `Expect.from_mapping` (`expect.py:640-669`). Purpose\n\nAuthor the **inputs + expects** body for eval E14 — the eleventh\npost-OQ-2 hook-surface coverage entry (R-086 … R-098), pinning the\nharness's **SessionStart concurrency contract** (design-spec §11 /\nOQ-2 D-0082 §4 row E14). E14 is the only entry in the v1 roster that\nexercises a **multi-session** surface: N=3 sessions spawned in rapid\nsuccession (within ~200ms of each other) MUST each write their own\nper-session sticky `state/auggie-first-pending/<sid>.txt` (no\ncross-contamination) and each MUST emit exactly one `session_init`\nevent in its own per-eval HOME's JSONL ledger.\n\nThe regression class motivating E14 is **shared-mutable-state at the\nSessionStart boundary**: a hook script or harness path that writes\nto a fixed location (no `<sid>` in the path) would have N=3 sessions\nrace on the same file, surfacing only the last writer's sticky and\ncorrupting per-session telemetry. E14 pins the no-shared-state\ninvariant.\n\nE14 is structurally distinct from E3 / E4 (which pin SINGLE-session\nSessionStart hook fire) along one axis: **N=3 multi-session\norchestration**. The OQ-2 contract (D-0082 §4 row E14 + D-4 decision\nnotes) names the v1 implementation mechanism as the **YAML\n`callback:` escape hatch** — a programmatic spawn ordering that\ncannot be expressed in the declarative input/expects DSL. The\ncallback (named `superclaude.cli.eval.suites.real_callbacks:\nE14_concurrent_session_start` per D-0082 §4) is responsible for:\n\n  (a) spawning N=3 sessions in threads with ~200ms inter-arrival;\n  (b) waiting for all 3 PTY sessions to reach prompt-ready;\n  (c) collecting the 3 per-session HOMEs' sticky files + JSONL\n      ledgers;\n  (d) asserting no-shared-mutable-state — 3 distinct sticky files,\n      3 distinct session_init JSONL rows, one per session.\n\nThe body must:\n\n- run on a fresh per-eval HOME isolated by FR-ISO2 (single-HOME\n  proxy; the FULL N=3 multi-HOME variant lives behind the callback\n  escape hatch);\n- assert the SessionStart-boundary observable shape — sticky-pending\n  directory created, session_init event row emitted, PTY teardown\n  exit 0 — mapped to the available declarative DSL primitives\n  (mapping documented in §3 and §8.1 below);\n- exit cleanly so `exit_code.equals(0)` can pin the PTY teardown\n  contract — sibling to E3-E13;\n- carry no capability tag (`requires: []`) — pure PTY spawn; no\n  MCP, no network. Matches OQ-2 D-0082 §6 row E14.\n\nThe \"YAML `callback:` field\" + the \"multi-session orchestration\"\ncannot be expressed in the current declarative DSL **or** the\ncurrent schema (`evalEntry.additionalProperties: false` rejects the\n`callback:` key). The strict form is deferred to follow-up tasks\nper the established T05.07..T05.19 precedent: land the OQ-2 body\nverbatim with the best-effort declarative N=1 proxy, document the\ngap in §8.1, and gate the strict form on the schema-bump + loader-\nextension + EvalRunner callback-invocation wiring described in\nD-4 / D-0082 §4.\n\n## 2. Concurrency contract (from design-spec §11 + OQ-2 D-0082 §4)\n\nThe harness's SessionStart hook chain (`session-init.sh` +\n`freshness-session-start.sh` per `src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json`)\nmust produce per-session output keyed by `$SESSION_ID`:\n\n```\nFor each spawned session i in {1..N=3}:\n  1. PTY spawn fires SessionStart hooks before prompt-ready.\n  2. session-init.sh writes state/session-init.log under <home_i>.\n  3. freshness-session-start.sh writes\n     state/auggie-first-pending/<sid_i>.txt under <home_i>.\n  4. session-init.sh (per OQ-2 contract) appends\n     `{\"type\":\"session_init\", \"sid\":<sid_i>, ...}` to\n     <home_i>/.claude/logs/session-events.jsonl.\n\nMulti-session invariants:\n  - Each home_i is a DISTINCT per-eval HOME (no cross-pollination).\n  - The <sid_i> values are pairwise distinct.\n  - Each logs/session-events.jsonl contains exactly ONE\n    session_init row (event_count == 1 per session).\n  - No state/auggie-first-pending/<sid_i>.txt appears under\n    home_j for i != j.\n```\n\nThe fixture spawn function (per OQ-2 D-0082 §4 row E14) is\n`superclaude.cli.eval.suites.real_callbacks:E14_concurrent_session_start`\n— a Python callable that uses threads + the PTY harness to drive\nthe 3 spawns and collects per-session observables for assertion.\n\n| Observable | Purpose |\n|---|---|\n| `state/auggie-first-pending/<sid>.txt` (per session) | proves the sticky-pending mechanism is keyed by `<sid>` (no shared-mutable-state) |\n| `logs/session-events.jsonl` (per session) contains `\"type\":\"session_init\"` | proves the session_init event row was emitted in EACH per-session ledger |\n| `event_count == 1` per session ledger | proves no duplicate emission (the SessionStart hook chain fires exactly once per spawn) |\n| `exit_code == 0` (per session) | proves clean PTY teardown for each spawn — the concurrent spawns don't corrupt each other's exit codepaths |\n\n## 3. Frozen body shape\n\nThe body lands in `suites/real.yaml` under the E14 entry that\npreviously carried only a stale placeholder (`title: \"doctor reports\nMCP gateway reachability\"`, `category: doctor` — left over from the\npre-OQ-2 numbering when E14 was a doctor-surface eval). T05.20\nreplaces the scaffolding with the frozen body. Final shape:\n\n| Field | Value |\n|---|---|\n| **title** | `\"Concurrent SessionStart bursts\"` (matches D-0082 §4 OQ-2 row E14) |\n| **category** | `hook-lifecycle` (sibling to E3-E13; was `doctor` in stale stub — the eval surface is the SessionStart hook chain at the multi-session boundary, which is part of the hook lifecycle, not the doctor diagnostics surface) |\n| **requires** | `[]` — no capability tags; the callback (once landed) spawns local PTY sessions only |\n| **timeout_sec** | `60` (matches E3-E13 sibling parity; in practice the degenerate N=1 proxy is bounded by 1× `/quit` through the PTY, typically <10s — 60s is generous headroom for the N=1 form. The full N=3 multi-session form would need a larger budget; that's a follow-up concern gated on the callback escape hatch landing.) |\n| **inputs[0].prompt** | `\"/quit\"` — fresh PTY session that fires the SessionStart hook chain; immediate clean exit. The N=3 concurrent-spawn aspect is fully deferred to the callback escape hatch (per §3 schema-expressibility constraint below). |\n| **expects[0]** | `file: { path: state/auggie-first-pending, exists: true }` — the sticky-pending directory created by `freshness-session-start.sh:91` on every SessionStart; necessary-but-not-sufficient proxy for \"each session writes its own `<sid>.txt`\" |\n| **expects[1]** | `file: { path: logs/session-events.jsonl, exists: true, contains: '\"type\":\"session_init\"' }` — single-session degenerate of \"3 distinct session_init events\"; sibling pattern to E3 |\n| **expects[2]** | `exit_code: { equals: 0 }` — clean PTY teardown |\n\nPTY-exclusion tag: `no_pty: skip` (carried forward from the\nscaffolding entry — every eval in the `real` suite is PTY-driven\nper DOC-OQ3 / R-077).\n\nNo additions to `optional_capabilities` — the callback (once\nlanded) is a pure Python thread-fan-out + PTY spawn; no MCP server\nis required.\n\n### Schema-expressibility constraint — the `callback:` field\n\nThe OQ-2 D-0082 §4 row E14 inputs shape names a **YAML `callback:`\nfield** that invokes\n`superclaude.cli.eval.suites.real_callbacks:E14_concurrent_session_start`\nper the D-4 escape hatch. The current `suites/suite.schema.json`\nshape for `evalEntry`:\n\n```json\n\"evalEntry\": {\n  \"required\": [\"id\", \"title\"],\n  \"additionalProperties\": false,\n  \"properties\": { \"id\": ..., \"title\": ..., \"category\": ...,\n                  \"requires\": ..., \"timeout_sec\": ...,\n                  \"isolation\": ..., \"inputs\": ..., \"expects\": ...,\n                  \"parameterize\": ..., \"no_pty\": ... }\n}\n```\n\n`additionalProperties: false` rejects any unrecognised field; the\nD-4 `callback:` escape hatch is documented in `decisions.md` (D-4)\nbut **not yet wired** into the schema, the loader, OR the EvalRunner.\nAdding the field end-to-end requires:\n\n  (a) `suite.schema.json` extension — add `callback: string` (or\n      `callback: { module: string, function: string }`) to\n      `evalEntry.properties`;\n  (b) loader update — `SuiteLoader` resolves the callable from the\n      `callback:` string by `importlib.import_module + getattr`;\n  (c) EvalRunner update — when an EvalSpec carries a `callback`, the\n      runner invokes it (instead of driving inputs through the PTY)\n      and consumes the callback's returned outcome shape;\n  (d) the `superclaude.cli.eval.suites.real_callbacks` module +\n      `E14_concurrent_session_start` function — actual multi-session\n      orchestration implementation.\n\nNone of (a)-(d) exist today. Per the established T05.07..T05.19\ndeferral posture, T05.20 lands the OQ-2 body shape with the\nbest-effort declarative proxy:\n\n- **N=3 → degenerate N=1.** A single PTY session (`inputs[0].prompt:\n  \"/quit\"`) fires the SessionStart hook chain, creates the sticky-\n  pending directory, and emits one session_init event — the same\n  observable shape E3 / E4 pin for SINGLE-session SessionStart. The\n  N=3 concurrency aspect (no-shared-mutable-state across parallel\n  spawns) is fully deferred to the callback escape hatch.\n- **per-`<sid>` sticky file → directory existence proxy.**\n  `Expect.file` (expect.py:187-268) has no glob support and no\n  `<sid>` template substitution — the per-session file\n  `state/auggie-first-pending/<sid>.txt` cannot be asserted without\n  knowing the session_id ahead of time. The directory\n  `state/auggie-first-pending/` (created by\n  `freshness-session-start.sh:91` on every SessionStart) is a\n  necessary-but-not-sufficient proxy: directory presence proves the\n  sticky-pending mechanism initialised; the actual per-session file\n  presence (and the no-cross-contamination guarantee across N=3\n  HOMEs) is deferred.\n- **3 distinct session_init events → 1 substring assertion.**\n  Inherits the same proxy posture as E3: `file(logs/session-events.jsonl,\n  contains '\"type\":\"session_init\"')`. The 3-events-across-3-HOMEs\n  aspect is deferred (multi-HOME assertion semantics not supported\n  — Expect.file resolves against a single EvalContext.home_path per\n  expect.py:187-268).\n- **`jsonl.event_count == 1` per session → degenerate-N=1 implicit.**\n  A single-session run produces exactly one session_init row by\n  construction; the per-session count=1 invariant for the multi-\n  session case is deferred to the callback escape hatch\n  (declarative `jsonl.event_count` requires a Python callable per\n  expect.py:269-369).\n\nThis proxy posture mirrors the deferral footprint established by\nT05.07..T05.19: lands the OQ-2 named ledger paths verbatim with\nsingle-session degenerate observables, defers the schema-bump-gated\nstrict form to follow-up tasks. T05.20 differs from prior bodies in\nONE structural respect: it depends on a schema EXTENSION (the\n`callback:` field) rather than only on a hook-script wiring (E3-E5)\nor a hooks.json variant + fixture (E13).\n\n### Scaffolding gap — five preconditions\n\nThe OQ-2 input shape requires:\n\n1. **The `callback:` field added to `suite.schema.json`** —\n   today's schema (`evalEntry.additionalProperties: false`) rejects\n   it.\n2. **The loader resolving `callback:` strings to callables** — no\n   import path resolution exists in `SuiteLoader`.\n3. **The EvalRunner invoking the callback** — `runner.py` drives\n   ONE PTY session per eval via `claude_process.py`; there is no\n   alternative invocation path that hands control to a Python\n   callable.\n4. **The `superclaude.cli.eval.suites.real_callbacks` module\n   existing** — the named callable `E14_concurrent_session_start`\n   has no implementation; the module directory itself does not\n   exist.\n5. **Multi-HOME isolation orchestration** — the per-eval setup\n   wrapper (NFR-ISO2 / T02.13) creates ONE HOME per eval; the\n   callback needs to create N=3 distinct HOMEs (one per concurrent\n   session) AND have Expect.file resolve assertions against ALL of\n   them (or have the callback collect per-session observables and\n   return them in a shape the reporter consumes).\n\nIn addition, T05.20 inherits two telemetry gaps from E3 / T05.07:\n\n6. **`session-init.sh` doesn't append to `logs/session-events.jsonl`\n   today** — the OQ-2 body names this ledger path; the current\n   `src/superclaude/hooks/scripts/session-init.sh` writes its own\n   `state/session-init.log` but does not emit the\n   `{\"type\":\"session_init\"}` JSONL row. Follow-up hook-script task\n   (shared with E3) is responsible for the emission.\n7. **`<sid>` keying on the sticky file is in\n   `freshness-session-start.sh:108`** — the script DOES write\n   `state/auggie-first-pending/$SESSION_ID.txt`, so the per-`<sid>`\n   keying is correct. But the proxy at expects[0] checks DIRECTORY\n   existence, not per-session file existence. The strict per-`<sid>`\n   assertion form is the deferral (gap #5 above).\n\nUntil gaps #1-#5 land, E14's full multi-session execution path is\nnot invocable. The degenerate N=1 proxy lands the OQ-2-named\nobservables (sticky-pending directory + session_init event +\nclean exit) so the regression-detection envelope is partially\ncovered TODAY: a regression that breaks the SessionStart hook\nchain entirely (no sticky directory, no JSONL ledger, non-zero\nPTY exit) would surface via the proxy. The strict no-shared-\nmutable-state assertion lives behind the callback escape hatch.\n\n§8.1 documents the deferral with the same \"schema-gap +\nimplementation-gap\" framing.\n\n### Why `inputs[0].prompt: \"/quit\"` (no `expect_tool_call`)\n\nThe N=1 proxy doesn't need to PIN a specific tool call (unlike E13\nwhich pinned `expect_tool_call: Read`). The SessionStart hooks fire\nBEFORE any tool call is issued — they fire as part of the PTY\nspawn handshake, not in response to any specific tool. The `/quit`\ninput is sufficient to trigger the full SessionStart chain and\nexit cleanly.\n\n## 4. Eval id passes FR-SCH2\n\n`validate_eval_id` (FR-SCH2 / T01.05) requires the eval id match\n`^[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9]*([0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?)?$`. The literal id `E14` is\ntrivially accepted — `eval describe --suite real --eval E14` returns\nthe full body and `eval list --json` continues to enumerate 17\nevals under suite `real`.\n\n## 5. Determinism (3-run AC)\n\nThe body is deterministic on a clean per-eval HOME (D-0082 §2\nconstraint 2 / per-task AC):\n\n- The SessionStart hook chain (session-init.sh +\n  freshness-session-start.sh) fires the same way on every PTY\n  spawn — same scripts, same matchers, same side-effects.\n- The `state/auggie-first-pending/` directory is created\n  unconditionally on every SessionStart (per\n  freshness-session-start.sh:91) — no time-of-day or network\n  dependencies.\n- The asserted substring `\"type\":\"session_init\"` (once the gap-#6\n  hook-script emission lands) is invariant across runs; the\n  `ts` / `session_id` fields on the JSONL row, once emitted, are\n  not asserted against.\n- The `/quit` input causes an immediate clean exit (exit code 0)\n  after PTY harness EOF.\n- No shared-state dependencies — D-0082 §2 constraint 3 (no\n  `CLAUDE_FAKE_TIME_OFFSET`) is honored.\n\nThree consecutive `eval run --suite real --eval E14` invocations\non a clean HOME must therefore yield identical EvalOutcome\nstatuses, which is the per-task acceptance criterion. (The\ndeterminism contract for the full N=3 multi-session form, once\nthe callback escape hatch lands, depends on the callback honoring\nthe same no-time-of-day, no-network, no-shared-state discipline —\nthe OQ-2 contract says \"spawned in rapid succession (within\n~200ms of each other) via threads\" which is deterministic modulo\nwall-clock skew. Same posture as standard thread-fan-out tests in\npytest.)\n\n## 6. Schema validation\n\nThe body uses only manifest-supported constructs:\n\n- `inputs[].prompt: string` (additionalProperties: true under\n  `evalEntry.inputs.items` per `suite.schema.json`).\n- `expects[]` rows matching `{primitive: kwargs}` shape — resolved\n  at load-time by `Expect.from_mapping` (`expect.py:640-669`). All\n  primitives used (2×`file`, 1×`exit_code`) are in `PRIMITIVE_NAMES`\n  (`expect.py:56-64`).\n- `file` primitive kwargs `path`, `exists`, `contains` are supported\n  by `Expect.file._build` (`expect.py:187-268`).\n- `exit_code` primitive kwarg `equals` is supported by\n  `Expect.exit_code._build`.\n- `requires: []` (empty array) is accepted by the schema.\n- `timeout_sec: 60` is `integer ≥ 1` per schema.\n- `isolation.home_strategy: ephemeral` is in the enum.\n- `no_pty: skip` matches the enum.\n\nNo schema-version bump required **for the proxy body**. The full\nOQ-2 shape with the `callback:` field requires a schema bump (see\n§3 / §8.1).\n\n## 7. `--no-mcp` and `--no-pty` behavior matrix\n\n| Invocation | E14 outcome | Why |\n|---|---|---|\n| `eval run --suite real --eval E14` (no flags) | RUNS (subject to §3 / §8.1 scaffolding gaps for full N=3 shape; the degenerate N=1 proxy runs) | no capability tags; PTY harness present on host |\n| `eval run --suite real --eval E14 --no-mcp` | RUNS (subject to §3) | `requires: []` — no MCP capability to skip |\n| `eval run --suite real --eval E14 --no-pty` | SKIPPED (`--no-pty`) | per-eval `no_pty: skip` tag (R-077 / D-0077); `--no-pty` short-circuits before any eval body executes |\n| `eval run --suite real --eval E14 --no-mcp --no-pty` | SKIPPED (`--no-pty`) | `--no-pty` short-circuits first per `commands.py` |\n\nThis posture matches siblings E3-E7 / E9-E12 / E13 / E15 (which all\ncarry `requires: []`) and differs from E1 / E2.1-3 / E8 (which\ncarry MCP capability tags and soft-skip under `--no-mcp`).\n\n## 8. Verification\n\nPer phase-5-tasklist.md T05.20, primary verifier:\n\n```bash\nuv run superclaude eval run --suite real --eval E14\n```\n\n**Today's runner state:** the same pre-existing `NameError: name\n'_new_run_id' is not defined` in `cli/eval/commands.py:1418`\ndocumented in T05.03..T05.19 evidence blocks all block any direct\n`eval run` invocation. That blocker is the responsibility of the\nrunner-completion task (Phase-5 dependency of the CP-P05-T19-T23\ncheckpoint at T05.24). T05.20 authors the manifest body;\nobservable verification is therefore via:\n\n- (a) `eval describe --suite real --eval E14` rendering the new\n  inputs/expects rows (manifest shape proof; see\n  `evidence/T05.20/describe-E14.txt`);\n- (b) `eval list --json` continuing to enumerate suite `real` with\n  17 evals (proves schema acceptance; see\n  `evidence/T05.20/list-with-E14.txt`);\n- (c) `Expect.from_mapping` round-trip over each `expects[]` row\n  (proves declarative DSL resolution; see\n  `evidence/T05.20/expect-roundtrip.txt`).\n\nFull end-to-end PTY execution + 3-run determinism proof rolls into\nthe runner-completion task downstream of T05.20, AND the\nscaffolding-gap closure tasks described in §8.1.\n\n### 8.1 Deferred branches — schema gap + multi-session orchestration\n\nOQ-2 D-0082 §4 row E14 specifies a four-part contract:\n\n1. **`callback:` invokes multi-session spawn function.** **Deferred** —\n   the schema rejects the `callback:` key today; loader and runner\n   have no callback resolution / invocation path; the named callable\n   has no implementation. See §3 \"Scaffolding gap\" gaps #1-#4.\n2. **Each session writes its own `state/auggie-first-pending/<sid>.txt`.**\n   Landed with the degenerate-N=1 directory-existence proxy\n   (`file.exists(state/auggie-first-pending)`). The per-`<sid>`\n   file presence + no-cross-contamination invariant across N=3\n   HOMEs is deferred to the callback escape hatch (Expect.file\n   resolves against a single EvalContext.home_path).\n3. **3 distinct session_init events in 3 separate JSONL files.**\n   Landed with the degenerate-N=1 single-substring proxy\n   (`file(logs/session-events.jsonl, contains '\"type\":\"session_init\"')`).\n   The 3-events-across-3-HOMEs aspect is deferred to the callback\n   escape hatch.\n4. **`jsonl.event_count == 1` per session.** Implicit in the N=1\n   proxy (one session produces exactly one row by construction);\n   the per-session count=1 invariant for the multi-session case is\n   deferred to the callback escape hatch (declarative\n   `jsonl.event_count` requires a Python callable per\n   expect.py:269-369).\n\nFollowing the same precedent as T05.07..T05.19 (telemetry gaps in\nhook scripts, event_count predicates not expressible declaratively,\n\"twice + digest\" deferrals, hooks.json-variant deployment gaps,\nstructured ledger emission gaps), T05.20 lands the OQ-2 body shape\nwith the best-effort declarative proxies for branches (2)-(4) and\ndefers branch (1) to follow-up tasks gated on:\n\n- (a) the `callback:` field added to `suites/suite.schema.json` —\n  schema bump + loader extension;\n- (b) the `superclaude.cli.eval.suites.real_callbacks` module\n  landing with the `E14_concurrent_session_start` function;\n- (c) the EvalRunner gaining an alternative invocation path that\n  resolves a callable from an EvalSpec.callback and drives the\n  multi-session orchestration;\n- (d) the multi-HOME isolation orchestration in\n  `home_isolation.py` / setup wrapper (N=3 distinct HOMEs per\n  callback invocation, with per-session observables collected for\n  assertion);\n- (e) (shared with E3 / T05.07) `session-init.sh` updated to\n  append `{\"type\":\"session_init\", ...}` rows to\n  `<home>/.claude/logs/session-events.jsonl`.\n\nThe proxy retains operational meaning: once (e) lands, the body\ncatches every regression in the **single-session** SessionStart\nhook chain — missing sticky-pending directory, missing\nsession_init event row, non-zero PTY teardown. The strict no-\nshared-mutable-state form (1)-(4) only adds discrimination against\nmulti-session regressions (a hook writing to a fixed\n`state/auggie-first-pending/global.txt` would race; the proxy\ncan't detect this).\n\nThis gap is **not introduced** by T05.20; it predates the\ndeliverable and is structural to the declarative DSL's\nexpressibility envelope PLUS the schema's current shape PLUS the\nharness's single-session-per-eval execution mode. Acceptance\ncriteria for T05.20 (manifest body landed, FR-SCH2-valid id, OQ-2\nbody shape recorded, spec/notes/evidence written) are met by the\ndescribe / list / roundtrip evidence above; the per-task AC that\nrequires `eval run --eval E14` to exit 0 deterministically depends\ntransitively on (i) the runner NameError fix and (ii)-(e) the\nfive scaffolding-gap closures listed in §3.\n\n### 8.2 Failure-mode taxonomy\n\n| Failure mode | Surface | Discriminator |\n|---|---|---|\n| Runner NameError unblocked, hook script doesn't emit session_init | eval FAIL on expects[1] | \"file does not contain '\\\"type\\\":\\\"session_init\\\"'\" — session-init.sh emission gap (#6 above) — shared with E3 / T05.07 |\n| Hook chain doesn't create sticky-pending dir | eval FAIL on expects[0] | \"file not found at state/auggie-first-pending\" — freshness-session-start.sh:91 regression |\n| Session_init emission wired, ledger ordering wrong | (proxy can't detect) | the strict per-`<sid>` form (deferred to callback) would catch this; proxy can't |\n| Multi-session shared-state regression (fixed-path sticky) | (proxy can't detect) | the strict form (deferred to callback) would catch this; the degenerate N=1 proxy can't differentiate single from multi |\n| PTY teardown breakage | eval FAIL on expects[2] | \"exit_code != 0\" — /quit didn't cleanly exit |\n\nThe expects[] ordering is deliberate: expects[0..1] surface the\nSessionStart hook chain observables (directory + ledger event)\nfirst; expects[2] discriminates the PTY teardown contract. The\nmulti-session-specific failure modes are NOT detectable by the\nproxy — they are the scaffolding-gap-deferred surface.\n\n## 9. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TckjFJy5F3CyYTCtTDpCz5","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# D-0098 — Notes / Design Rationale\n2\t\n3\t## Why E14's surface is multi-session concurrency, not a single hook\n4\t\n5\tE3-E11 each pin one row of `src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json` — the\n6\teval drives Claude Code through the PTY, fires the matched hook, and\n7\treads the freshness ledger to prove the hook script emitted its\n8\tevent. E12 pins the **deployer** (`hook_adapter.deploy_hooks_to`).\n9\tE13 pins the **harness's hook-execution error-handling path**. E14\n10\tis structurally distinct from all of them: its surface is the\n11\t**SessionStart hook chain at the multi-session boundary** — the\n12\tshared-mutable-state regression class that arises when N concurrent\n13\tsessions fire SessionStart hooks within a small time window.\n14\t\n15\tThe body is intentionally hook-event-agnostic on the strict form:\n16\tthe OQ-2 contract (D-0082 §4 row E14) names per-session\n17\t`state/auggie-first-pending/<sid>.txt` (sticky), per-session\n18\t`logs/session-events.jsonl` (session_init), and per-session\n19\t`event_count == 1`. The regression class motivating E14 is **any\n20\thook script or harness path that writes to a fixed location (no\n21\t`<sid>` in the path)**: such a path would have N=3 sessions race on\n22\tthe same file, surfacing only the last writer's sticky and\n23\tcorrupting per-session telemetry.\n24\t\n25\tThe natural strict body shape is: spawn N=3 PTY sessions in rapid\n26\tsuccession (~200ms inter-arrival), wait for all 3 to reach\n27\tprompt-ready, then assert four observable guarantees of the\n28\tno-shared-mutable-state contract:\n29\t\n30\t1. Each session's per-eval HOME contains its own\n31\t   `state/auggie-first-pending/<sid>.txt` (no cross-contamination).\n32\t2. Each session's per-eval HOME contains exactly one session_init\n33\t   event in its `logs/session-events.jsonl` ledger.\n34\t3. The `<sid>` values are pairwise distinct (no SESSION_ID reuse).\n35\t4. Each PTY teardown exits cleanly (no inter-process corruption).\n36\t\n37\tThat's the OQ-2 contract verbatim. The body assertions ATTEMPTED to\n38\tpin all four guarantees with the best-effort declarative proxies,\n39\tbut the multi-session orchestration itself is unexpressible in the\n40\tcurrent declarative DSL — it requires a Python callable that\n41\tthreads spawn fan-out + per-session HOME collection. D-4's YAML\n42\t`callback:` escape hatch is the named vehicle, deferred to follow-up\n43\ttasks gated on schema + loader + runner extensions.\n44\t\n45\t## Why the declarative DSL can't express N=3 multi-session\n46\t\n47\tThe DSL is single-input, single-PTY-spawn, single-HOME by design:\n48\t\n49\t- `inputs[]` enumerates a SEQUENCE of prompts driven through ONE\n50\t  PTY in order (per `EvalRunner._execute_eval` driving\n51\t  `claude_process.send_input` per-input).\n52\t- `EvalContext.home_path` is a SINGLE per-eval HOME directory\n53\t  created by the setup wrapper (NFR-ISO2 / T02.13) — not a list of\n54\t  N HOMEs.\n55\t- `Expect.file(path=..., exists=..., contains=...)` resolves `path`\n56\t  against `EvalContext.home_path` per `expect.py:187-268` — no\n57\t  multi-HOME assertion semantics.\n58\t\n59\tTo express N=3 multi-session concurrency, the runner must:\n60\t\n61\t1. Detect an alternative invocation mode (callback vs. PTY-driven).\n62\t2. Hand control to a Python callable instead of driving inputs.\n63\t3. Provide the callable with primitives to spawn isolated PTY\n64\t   sessions in parallel (N distinct HOMEs).\n65\t4. Collect per-session observables (sticky files, JSONL rows, exit\n66\t   codes) and return them in a shape the reporter consumes.\n67\t\n68\tNone of those orchestration primitives exist today. The current\n69\trunner is single-PTY-per-eval, structurally. The OQ-2 D-0082 §4\n70\trow E14 explicitly names the v1 implementation mechanism as the\n71\t**YAML `callback:` field**, which (a) needs to land in the schema,\n72\t(b) needs loader-side string→callable resolution, (c) needs\n73\trunner-side callback invocation, (d) needs the named callable to\n74\texist, and (e) needs multi-HOME orchestration in the setup wrapper.\n75\tNone of (a)-(e) exist today; T05.20 lands the body that names the\n76\tcontract and depends on them.\n77\t\n78\t## Why the proxy posture is \"degenerate N=1\" (single PTY session)\n79\t\n80\tGiven the five preconditions above are not met, T05.20's options\n81\twere:\n82\t\n83\t- **Option A: Land an OQ-2-shaped body with N=3 multi-session\n84\t  orchestration via the callback escape hatch.** Rejected — the\n85\t  callback field isn't schema-valid, the loader doesn't resolve it,\n86\t  the runner doesn't invoke it, and the named callable doesn't\n87\t  exist. Landing this body would produce a schema validation error\n88\t  in `SuiteLoader.load`, blocking the entire suite from\n89\t  enumerating.\n90\t- **Option B: Land a degenerate N=1 body with single-PTY-session\n91\t  observables (sticky-pending directory + session_init ledger event\n92\t  + clean PTY exit).** Selected — the body satisfies FR-SCH2 (id\n93\t  matches regex), the schema accepts the entry (no `callback:`\n94\t  field), `Expect.from_mapping` resolves all 3 expects cleanly, and\n95\t  the suite continues to enumerate. The body's assertions are\n96\t  necessary-but-not-sufficient for the OQ-2 contract: they catch\n97\t  single-session SessionStart regressions but cannot catch\n98\t  multi-session shared-state regressions.\n99\t- **Option C: Skip the eval entirely (no body) until the callback\n100\t  escape hatch lands.** Rejected — the OQ-2 resolution explicitly\n101\t  scheduled E14 for T05.20 authoring; skipping would violate the\n102\t  per-task acceptance criteria (\"File `suites/real.yaml` contains\n103\t  entry `id: E14` matching the OQ-2 resolution\").\n104\t\n105\tOption B mirrors the deferral posture established by T05.07..T05.19\n106\t(land OQ-2-named observables with declarative proxies; document\n107\tscaffolding gaps without introducing them).\n108\t\n109\t## Why `state/auggie-first-pending` (directory, not per-`<sid>` file)\n110\t\n111\t`freshness-session-start.sh:91` creates the directory\n112\t`$STATE_DIR/auggie-first-pending` unconditionally on every\n113\tSessionStart, then `:108` writes\n114\t`$STATE_DIR/auggie-first-pending/$SESSION_ID.txt` to mark the\n115\tsession as auggie-first-pending. The OQ-2 contract names the\n116\tper-`<sid>` file as the asserted observable.\n117\t\n118\t`Expect.file(path=..., exists=true)` (`expect.py:187-268`) does:\n119\t\n120\t- string `path` resolution against `EvalContext.home_path`;\n121\t- single-file existence check via `Path.exists()`;\n122\t- no glob support, no template substitution, no `<sid>` placeholder\n123\t  resolution (the eval doesn't know the session_id ahead of time —\n124\t  it's generated by the PTY harness at spawn).\n125\t\n126\tThe two options for asserting the per-`<sid>` file:\n127\t\n128\t- **(a) Glob-based `Expect.file_glob` primitive** — would let the\n129\t  body say `file_glob(state/auggie-first-pending/*.txt,\n130\t  exists: true)`. Doesn't exist today; would need a new primitive\n131\t  in `expect.py` + schema entry.\n132\t- **(b) Callback-collected per-session observable** — the\n133\t  E14_concurrent_session_start callback would collect each\n134\t  session's actual sticky file path and assert directly. Doesn't\n135\t  exist today (gaps #1-#5).\n136\t\n137\tT05.20 falls back to **directory existence**: the directory IS\n138\tcreated by `freshness-session-start.sh:91` on every SessionStart,\n139\tso presence of the directory proves the sticky-pending mechanism\n140\tinitialised. This is **necessary-but-not-sufficient** for the\n141\tper-`<sid>` form: a hypothetical regression where\n142\t`freshness-session-start.sh` creates the directory but fails to\n143\twrite any `<sid>.txt` file would satisfy the proxy and violate the\n144\tstrict semantic. D-0098 — Notes / Design Rationale\n\n## Why E14's surface is multi-session concurrency, not a single hook\n\nE3-E11 each pin one row of `src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json` — the\neval drives Claude Code through the PTY, fires the matched hook, and\nreads the freshness ledger to prove the hook script emitted its\nevent. E12 pins the **deployer** (`hook_adapter.deploy_hooks_to`).\nE13 pins the **harness's hook-execution error-handling path**. E14\nis structurally distinct from all of them: its surface is the\n**SessionStart hook chain at the multi-session boundary** — the\nshared-mutable-state regression class that arises when N concurrent\nsessions fire SessionStart hooks within a small time window.\n\nThe body is intentionally hook-event-agnostic on the strict form:\nthe OQ-2 contract (D-0082 §4 row E14) names per-session\n`state/auggie-first-pending/<sid>.txt` (sticky), per-session\n`logs/session-events.jsonl` (session_init), and per-session\n`event_count == 1`. The regression class motivating E14 is **any\nhook script or harness path that writes to a fixed location (no\n`<sid>` in the path)**: such a path would have N=3 sessions race on\nthe same file, surfacing only the last writer's sticky and\ncorrupting per-session telemetry.\n\nThe natural strict body shape is: spawn N=3 PTY sessions in rapid\nsuccession (~200ms inter-arrival), wait for all 3 to reach\nprompt-ready, then assert four observable guarantees of the\nno-shared-mutable-state contract:\n\n1. Each session's per-eval HOME contains its own\n   `state/auggie-first-pending/<sid>.txt` (no cross-contamination).\n2. Each session's per-eval HOME contains exactly one session_init\n   event in its `logs/session-events.jsonl` ledger.\n3. The `<sid>` values are pairwise distinct (no SESSION_ID reuse).\n4. Each PTY teardown exits cleanly (no inter-process corruption).\n\nThat's the OQ-2 contract verbatim. The body assertions ATTEMPTED to\npin all four guarantees with the best-effort declarative proxies,\nbut the multi-session orchestration itself is unexpressible in the\ncurrent declarative DSL — it requires a Python callable that\nthreads spawn fan-out + per-session HOME collection. D-4's YAML\n`callback:` escape hatch is the named vehicle, deferred to follow-up\ntasks gated on schema + loader + runner extensions.\n\n## Why the declarative DSL can't express N=3 multi-session\n\nThe DSL is single-input, single-PTY-spawn, single-HOME by design:\n\n- `inputs[]` enumerates a SEQUENCE of prompts driven through ONE\n  PTY in order (per `EvalRunner._execute_eval` driving\n  `claude_process.send_input` per-input).\n- `EvalContext.home_path` is a SINGLE per-eval HOME directory\n  created by the setup wrapper (NFR-ISO2 / T02.13) — not a list of\n  N HOMEs.\n- `Expect.file(path=..., exists=..., contains=...)` resolves `path`\n  against `EvalContext.home_path` per `expect.py:187-268` — no\n  multi-HOME assertion semantics.\n\nTo express N=3 multi-session concurrency, the runner must:\n\n1. Detect an alternative invocation mode (callback vs. PTY-driven).\n2. Hand control to a Python callable instead of driving inputs.\n3. Provide the callable with primitives to spawn isolated PTY\n   sessions in parallel (N distinct HOMEs).\n4. Collect per-session observables (sticky files, JSONL rows, exit\n   codes) and return them in a shape the reporter consumes.\n\nNone of those orchestration primitives exist today. The current\nrunner is single-PTY-per-eval, structurally. The OQ-2 D-0082 §4\nrow E14 explicitly names the v1 implementation mechanism as the\n**YAML `callback:` field**, which (a) needs to land in the schema,\n(b) needs loader-side string→callable resolution, (c) needs\nrunner-side callback invocation, (d) needs the named callable to\nexist, and (e) needs multi-HOME orchestration in the setup wrapper.\nNone of (a)-(e) exist today; T05.20 lands the body that names the\ncontract and depends on them.\n\n## Why the proxy posture is \"degenerate N=1\" (single PTY session)\n\nGiven the five preconditions above are not met, T05.20's options\nwere:\n\n- **Option A: Land an OQ-2-shaped body with N=3 multi-session\n  orchestration via the callback escape hatch.** Rejected — the\n  callback field isn't schema-valid, the loader doesn't resolve it,\n  the runner doesn't invoke it, and the named callable doesn't\n  exist. Landing this body would produce a schema validation error\n  in `SuiteLoader.load`, blocking the entire suite from\n  enumerating.\n- **Option B: Land a degenerate N=1 body with single-PTY-session\n  observables (sticky-pending directory + session_init ledger event\n  + clean PTY exit).** Selected — the body satisfies FR-SCH2 (id\n  matches regex), the schema accepts the entry (no `callback:`\n  field), `Expect.from_mapping` resolves all 3 expects cleanly, and\n  the suite continues to enumerate. The body's assertions are\n  necessary-but-not-sufficient for the OQ-2 contract: they catch\n  single-session SessionStart regressions but cannot catch\n  multi-session shared-state regressions.\n- **Option C: Skip the eval entirely (no body) until the callback\n  escape hatch lands.** Rejected — the OQ-2 resolution explicitly\n  scheduled E14 for T05.20 authoring; skipping would violate the\n  per-task acceptance criteria (\"File `suites/real.yaml` contains\n  entry `id: E14` matching the OQ-2 resolution\").\n\nOption B mirrors the deferral posture established by T05.07..T05.19\n(land OQ-2-named observables with declarative proxies; document\nscaffolding gaps without introducing them).\n\n## Why `state/auggie-first-pending` (directory, not per-`<sid>` file)\n\n`freshness-session-start.sh:91` creates the directory\n`$STATE_DIR/auggie-first-pending` unconditionally on every\nSessionStart, then `:108` writes\n`$STATE_DIR/auggie-first-pending/$SESSION_ID.txt` to mark the\nsession as auggie-first-pending. The OQ-2 contract names the\nper-`<sid>` file as the asserted observable.\n\n`Expect.file(path=..., exists=true)` (`expect.py:187-268`) does:\n\n- string `path` resolution against `EvalContext.home_path`;\n- single-file existence check via `Path.exists()`;\n- no glob support, no template substitution, no `<sid>` placeholder\n  resolution (the eval doesn't know the session_id ahead of time —\n  it's generated by the PTY harness at spawn).\n\nThe two options for asserting the per-`<sid>` file:\n\n- **(a) Glob-based `Expect.file_glob` primitive** — would let the\n  body say `file_glob(state/auggie-first-pending/*.txt,\n  exists: true)`. Doesn't exist today; would need a new primitive\n  in `expect.py` + schema entry.\n- **(b) Callback-collected per-session observable** — the\n  E14_concurrent_session_start callback would collect each\n  session's actual sticky file path and assert directly. Doesn't\n  exist today (gaps #1-#5).\n\nT05.20 falls back to **directory existence**: the directory IS\ncreated by `freshness-session-start.sh:91` on every SessionStart,\nso presence of the directory proves the sticky-pending mechanism\ninitialised. This is **necessary-but-not-sufficient** for the\nper-`<sid>` form: a hypothetical regression where\n`freshness-session-start.sh` creates the directory but fails to\nwrite any `<sid>.txt` file would satisfy the proxy and violate the\nstrict semantic. In practice the two lines (`:91 mkdir` + `:108 :>\nsid.txt`) are co-located in the script, so the proxy converges with\nthe strict semantic.\n\n## Why `logs/session-events.jsonl` substring (not 3× row count)\n\nThe OQ-2 contract names \"3 distinct session_init events in 3\nseparate JSONL files\". The N=1 proxy collapses this to \"1\nsession_init event in 1 JSONL file\":\n`file(logs/session-events.jsonl, contains '\"type\":\"session_init\"')`.\nThe proxy:\n\n- proves the SessionStart hook chain emitted a session_init row to\n  the per-eval HOME's ledger;\n- does NOT prove the event_count==1 invariant (declarative\n  `jsonl.event_count` needs a Python callable, deferred to the\n  callback);\n- does NOT prove the 3-across-3-HOMEs no-cross-contamination\n  invariant (multi-HOME assertion isn't expressible without\n  callbacks).\n\nThis proxy is shared with E3 / T05.07 — both inherit the\n**hook-script emission gap** (`session-init.sh` doesn't yet append\n`{\"type\":\"session_init\", ...}` rows to `logs/session-events.jsonl`).\nOnce that gap closes (shared follow-up with E3), the proxy catches\nthe single-session emission regression for E14; the multi-session\ndiscrimination remains gated on the callback.\n\n## Why `exit_code: equals 0` (not multi-session exit code matrix)\n\nThe OQ-2 contract says each of N=3 PTY teardowns must exit cleanly.\nThe N=1 proxy asserts ONE PTY exit code. Sibling parity with\nE3-E13 / E15 (all assert `exit_code.equals(0)` against the single\nPTY teardown).\n\nThe multi-session exit-code matrix (3 exit codes, all == 0) is\nimplicit in the callback's per-session observable collection: the\ncallback would return a per-session list of exit codes, and the\nreporter would assert all match 0. Deferred to the callback.\n\n## Why `category: hook-lifecycle` (sibling parity)\n\nThe stale E14 placeholder carried `category: doctor` (a leftover\nfrom the pre-OQ-2 numbering when E14 was \"doctor reports MCP\ngateway reachability\"). The OQ-2 resolution reassigned E14 to the\nSessionStart multi-session boundary, which is part of the hook\nlifecycle. The category options:\n\n- `harness` or `concurrency` — technically accurate (the surface IS\n  the harness's multi-session orchestration boundary), but no other\n  eval in the suite uses these categories; creating singleton\n  categories creates noise in `eval list` filtering.\n- `doctor` — wrong (E14 doesn't exercise any doctor surface).\n- `hook-lifecycle` — the sibling category for E3-E13. E14 covers\n  the multi-session boundary of the SessionStart hook chain\n  (sticky-pending + session_init telemetry); E3 / E4 cover the\n  single-session boundary. The category groups them together for\n  filtering and reporting purposes.\n\nT05.20 picks `hook-lifecycle` for sibling parity, matching the\nT05.07 / E3 + T05.19 / E13 precedent.\n\n## Why `requires: []` (not `[mcp_server.*]`)\n\nThe callback (once landed) spawns local PTY sessions only — no\nnetwork, no MCP servers, no external binaries beyond the standard\n`claude` CLI binary that the PTY harness already drives. The\ndegenerate N=1 proxy is even simpler: a single `/quit` through one\nPTY. Per D-0082 §6 capability-tag rollup, E14's row lists no\ncapability tag.\n\nThe practical implication: E14 runs under `--no-mcp` (the\nmatcher-coverage gate counts it as a non-MCP eval), and the only\nway E14 skips is via `--no-pty` (per-eval `no_pty: skip` tag).\nMatches siblings E3-E7 / E9-E12 / E13 / E15.\n\n## What this body does NOT assert (and why)\n\nThe OQ-2 D-0082 §4 row E14 frames four explicit observable\ncontracts:\n\n1. **N=3 sessions spawned in rapid succession** — NOT asserted in\n   the proxy (single PTY session). Deferred to callback.\n2. **Each session writes its own `<sid>.txt` sticky (no\n   cross-contamination)** — partially asserted via directory\n   existence; per-`<sid>` form deferred to callback.\n3. **3 distinct session_init events in 3 separate JSONL files** —\n   partially asserted via single-substring presence; multi-HOME\n   form deferred to callback.\n4. **`event_count == 1` per session** — implicit in N=1; per-session\n   form deferred to callback.\n\nPlus one inherited gap from E3 / T05.07:\n\n5. **`session-init.sh` doesn't append to `logs/session-events.jsonl`\n   today** — the OQ-2 body names this ledger path; the current\n   hook script writes `state/session-init.log` but does not emit\n   the `{\"type\":\"session_init\"}` JSONL row. The design contract (design-spec §11 / OQ-2\n    # D-0082 §4 row E15) requires the harness to:\n    #   (a) reap the slow hook at the configured timeout boundary — the\n    #       tool call MUST complete within `hook_timeout + 2.0` seconds\n    #       (the +2.0s grace covers reap-overhead, not steady-state slop);\n    #   (b) complete the matched tool call successfully despite the\n    #       hook timeout — the tool's result reaches the agent (fail-\n    #       open disposition);\n    #   (c) emit a structured `{type:\"hook_timeout\", disposition:\n    #       \"fail_open\"}` row to `logs/hook-errors.jsonl` so the timeout\n    #       event is auditable post-run.\n    # E15 sits in `hook-lifecycle` category alongside E3-E13 — it pins\n    # the timeout-handling branch of the error-half of the lifecycle,\n    # parallel to E13's stderr-error branch. Together E13 (non-zero exit\n    # + stderr) and E15 (timeout) form the complete failure-mode\n    # coverage of the hook fail-open contract from design-spec §11.\n    #\n    # OQ-2-frozen body shape (D-0082 §4 row E15 / decisions.md OQ-2):\n    #   - inputs[0]: Use the Write tool to seed a scratch fixture\n    #     (`fixture.txt`) so the subsequent Read has a target. The OQ-2\n    #     contract names this as \"inject prompt triggering a Read\":\n    #     the Read is the matched tool call whose PostToolUse hook\n    #     script (the slow fixture per OQ-2 spec —\n    #     `tests/fixtures/hooks/slow-post-read.sh`) sleeps longer than\n    #     the configured `timeout:` field. The harness MUST fail open:\n    #     the Read tool's result reaches the agent successfully within\n    #     the timeout reap window, and the hook timeout is logged\n    #     separately.\n    #   - inputs[2]: prompt \"/quit\" — clean session exit so\n    #     `exit_code.equals(0)` can pin the PTY teardown contract\n    #     (sibling pattern to E3 / E4 / E5 / E6 / E7 / E8 / E9 / E10 /\n    #     E11 / E12 / E13).\n    #   - expects[0]: file.exists(logs/hook-errors.jsonl) — proves\n    #     the hook-error ledger was opened by the harness when the\n    #     PostToolUse hook timed out. This is the EXIT CODE OF THE EVAL PROCESS (the PTY\n    #     teardown contract), not the timeout-reap status of the slow\n    #     hook script. The fail-open contract is what makes these\n    #     decouple: the hook is reaped at the timeout boundary, the\n    #     harness logs the timeout and continues, and the overall\n    #     session exits 0 on /quit.\n    #\n    # No capability tags; no MCP; no network — the slow fixture is a\n    # pure shell script (`sleep N`) + the harness's timeout-reap path\n    # is pure stdlib (subprocess timeout / SIGTERM handling). E15\n    # carries `requires: []` and runs under `--no-mcp`. The regression class motivating E14 is:\n    # shared-mutable-state at SessionStart — a hook script or harness\n    # path that writes to a fixed location (no <sid> in the path) would\n    # have N=3 sessions race on the same file, surfacing only the last\n    # writer's sticky and corrupting the per-session telemetry.\n    #\n    # E14 is structurally distinct from E3 / E4 (which pin SINGLE-session\n    # SessionStart hook fire) along one axis: N=3 multi-session\n    # orchestration. The\n    # callback (named `superclaude.cli.eval.suites.real_callbacks:\n    # E14_concurrent_session_start` per D-0082 §4) is responsible for:\n    #   (a) spawning N=3 sessions in threads with ~200ms inter-arrival;\n    #   (b) waiting for all 3 PTY sessions to reach prompt-ready;\n    #   (c) collecting the 3 per-session HOMEs' sticky files + JSONL\n    #       ledgers;\n    #   (d) asserting no-shared-mutable-state — 3 distinct sticky\n    #       files, 3 distinct session_init JSONL rows, one per session.\n    #\n    # OQ-2-frozen body shape (D-0082 §4 row E14 / decisions.md OQ-2):\n    #   - inputs: YAML `callback:` field invokes the multi-session\n    #     spawn function (per D-4 escape hatch).\n    #   - expects: each session writes `state/auggie-first-pending/\n    #     <sid>.txt`; 3 distinct session_init events in 3 separate\n    #     JSONL files (one per session HOME); `jsonl.event_count == 1`\n    #     per session — proves no duplicate emission and no shared\n    #     mutable state across the concurrent spawns.\n    #\n    # Schema-expressibility constraint — the `callback:` field is NOT\n    # present on `evalEntry` in `suites/suite.schema.json` today\n    # (`additionalProperties: false` rejects any unrecognised field,\n    # and D-4's `callback:` escape hatch is documented in\n    # decisions.md but not yet wired into the schema). E13 and E15 form a matched pair — both pin the\nfail-open contract, but discriminated by the failure mode:\n\n| Eval | Failure trigger | Fixture script (deferred) | Ledger discriminator | Unique expects |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| E13 | hook exits non-zero with stderr | `tests/fixtures/hooks/failing-post-read.sh` | `\"type\":\"hook_error\"` | `stderr.contains(\"failing-post-read.sh\")` |\n| E15 | hook sleeps past `timeout:` field | `tests/fixtures/hooks/slow-post-read.sh` | `\"type\":\"hook_timeout\"` | (strict) `duration.less_than(hook_timeout + 2.0)` |\n\nThe two evals share three of four scaffolding gaps (fixture script,\nhooks.json-variant deployment, structured ledger emission). The inheritance stack from\nT05.07 (D-0087) through T05.20 (D-0098) has built up the following\nconsolidated follow-up roster, ordered by how many evals each gap blocks:\n\n| Scaffolding gap | Blocks evals | First identified | Follow-up class |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Hook scripts don't emit `{\"type\":\"session_init\"}` rows to `logs/session-events.jsonl` | E3 (T05.07), E14 (T05.20) | T05.07 | hook-script wiring |\n| `logs/freshness.jsonl` per-matcher row emission | E4-E11 | T05.08..T05.16 | hook-script wiring |\n| `hooks.json` variant deployment path (`isolation.hooks_variant:` field) | **E13 (T05.19), E15 (T05.21)** | T05.19 | schema bump + setup-wrapper |\n| Structured `logs/hook-errors.jsonl` emission | **E13 (T05.19), E15 (T05.21)** | T05.19 | harness extension |\n| YAML `callback:` schema field + loader + runner wiring | E14 (T05.20) | T05.20 | schema bump + loader + runner |\n| `tests/fixtures/hooks/failing-post-read.sh` fixture | E13 (T05.19) | T05.19 | fixture creation |\n| `tests/fixtures/hooks/slow-post-read.sh` fixture | E15 (T05.21) | T05.21 | fixture creation |\n| Expect.duration primitive | **E15 (T05.21) only** | T05.21 | Expect.* extension |\n\nT05.21 inherits **4 scaffolding gaps** (3 shared with E13 + 1 unique\nduration primitive). This is one less than T05.20 (5 gaps) because T05.21\nreuses E13's hooks.json-variant + ledger-emission tracks rather than\nintroducing a fresh dependency (E14's callback-escape-hatch was novel).\n\n## Why a body-verbatim proxy posture (not a downsized body)\n\nThe OQ-2 contract is a stable target for the post-Phase-5 harness work\nto converge on. Landing a downsized body (e.g. omitting the\n`type:\"hook_timeout\"` discriminator row to \"make the proxy pass today\")\nwould (a) make the body diverge from the OQ-2 contract, requiring an\nedit when the harness scaffolding lands, and (b) lose the auditability\nof the deferral — a future contributor reading `real.yaml` would not\nsee what the strict form requires.\n\nThe body-verbatim + proxy-substring posture solves both: the body\nmirrors the OQ-2 expects shape one-for-one (4 rows, conjunctive\nsubstring filter over the same JSONL file), and the strict form lands\nwhen the scaffolding closes — no body edit needed, only Expect.duration\naddition for the orthogonal wall-clock axis.\n\n## Why two-substring conjunction over the same file\n\nThe OQ-2 contract names `jsonl.contains_event(..., type=hook_timeout,\ndisposition=fail_open)` as a single keyword-arg-filtered predicate.\nDeclarative YAML cannot express keyword-arg predicates against\nExpect.jsonl (which routes to a Python callable per `expect.py:269-369`\nfor predicate dispatch). The Expect.file primitive's `contains:` field\ntakes a single substring per row.\n\nThe proxy decomposes the conjunction into two rows: one substring per\ndiscriminator, both against the same `logs/hook-errors.jsonl` file.\nThis mirrors the E13 (T05.19) precedent and the E6/E7/E8 sibling pattern\n(two-substring `{type, matcher}` conjunction). The limitation: the two\nsubstrings need not appear on the SAME row of the JSONL file — a\npathological harness that emitted them on different rows would still\npass the proxy. This is captured in spec.md §8.2 (\"concurrent hook\ntimeouts garble ledger rows\").\n\n## Why /quit is the third input\n\n`exit_code.equals: 0` pins the PTY teardown contract, NOT the\ntimeout-reap status of the slow hook. This decouples two assertions\nthat the fail-open contract makes orthogonal: the hook timeouts, the\nharness reaps + logs, the tool call completes, and the session exits\n0 on `/quit` regardless. A naive harness that propagated the hook\ntimeout to the session exit code would surface as `exit_code` ≠ 0,\nwhich would FAIL this expect — so `/quit` provides a discriminator\nbetween fail-open (exit 0) and fail-closed (non-zero exit propagated).\n\n## Why no `expect_tool_call` on inputs[2]\n\nThe `/quit` input is a session-control command, not a Read/Write/Edit\ntool invocation; it doesn't fire a tool-call hook. The\n`expect_tool_call: Read` annotation on inputs[1] pins the Read tool as\nthe trigger for the slow PostToolUse hook (the OQ-2 contract names\n\"inject prompt triggering a Read\"). Matches the E13/T05.19 pattern\nexactly.\n\n## Final-body retrospective\n\nT05.21 is the seventeenth and last evalbody to land under the OQ-2\nresolution. The 17-eval roster is now schema-complete: E1, E2.1-2.3,\nE3-E15.
